reports</h2><p class="fe-text fe-console__promise">Everything the dashboard does is an API call, and everything that happens to your mail can be pushed to your systems.</p><ul class="fe-grid fe-grid--2 fe-console__cards"><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">REST API</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Domains, aliases, messages, logs</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">Webhooks</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Inbound mail as JSON events</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">DMARC reports</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Parsed, aggregated, human-readable</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">Deliverability</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Per-domain analytics and alerts</span></li></ul><p class="fe-console__evidence fe-code"><span>POST https://your.app/hooks/mail → 200 ·</span><span>1,284 events today</span></p></section><section class="fe-console__view fe-console__view--primary" id="apps" aria-labelledby="view-apps-title" data-fe-console-view="apps"><p class="fe-console__kicker"><span class="fe-console__dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>Use</p><h2 class="fe-console__heading" id="view-apps-title">Desktop, mobile and terminal apps</h2><p class="fe-text fe-console__promise">Desktop and mobile apps built by us, with encrypted local storage, offline-first, auto-updating, and the same app in your terminal. Or keep using Apple Mail; both work.</p><ul class="fe-grid fe-grid--2 fe-console__cards"><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">macOS · Windows · Linux</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Signed, notarized, auto-updating</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">iOS · Android</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Native apps with push notifications</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">Terminal</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">The forwardemail command, with desktop notifications</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">App Lock</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Local cache encrypted, locked behind biometrics</span></li></ul><p class="fe-console__evidence fe-code"><span class="fe-console__evidence-glyph" aria-hidden="true">✓</span><span>update check → up to date ·</span><span>signature verified</span></p></section></div></div></div></div></div></section><section class="fe-section fe-surface-light fe-section--pad fe-product" aria-labelledby="fe-product-title"><div class="fe-container"><p class="fe-eyebrow fe-label"><i class="fa fa-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>All-in-one, no add-on fees</p><div class="fe-product__head"><h2 class="fe-display-lg" id="fe-product-title">One control plane for forwarding, mailboxes, sending, and deliverability.</h2><ul class="fe-stats fe-product__stats"><li><span class="fe-stats__value fe-numeral">99.99%</span><span class="fe-stats__label fe-label">Uptime</span></li><li><span class="fe-stats__value fe-numeral">10 GB</span><span class="fe-stats__label fe-label">Pooled storage</span></li><li><span class="fe-stats__value fe-numeral">$0</span><span class="fe-stats__label fe-label">Per alias</span></li></ul></div><div class="fe-product__intro"><p class="fe-lede">You run forwarding, mailboxes, sending and deliverability for your domain from one dashboard. Cure53 has audited our code twice.</p><ul class="fe-chips"><li class="fe-chip"><i class="fab fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i>Open-source</li><li class="fe-chip"><i class="fa fa-lock fe-chip__icon--success" aria-hidden="true"></i>PGP forwarding</li><li class="fe-chip"><i class="fa fa-shield-alt fe-chip__icon--primary" aria-hidden="true"></i>Spam + phishing guard</li></ul></div><div class="fe-feature fe-feature--inverse fe-surface-dark"><h3 class="fe-display-sm fe-feature__title">Built for developers</h3><p class="fe-text fe-feature__body">Everything the dashboard does is an API call. Our webmail and apps use the same API. <a class="fe-feature__link" href="/en/email-api">Read the API reference</a></p><div class="fe-codeblock"><pre><code><span class="fe-tok-method">POST /v1/emails</span>␊

    <span class="fe-tok-result">→ 202 Accepted · signed · tracked</span></code></pre></div><ul class="fe-chips"><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">REST API</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">SMTP</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">IMAP</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">POP3</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">CalDAV</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">CardDAV</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">Webhooks</li></ul></div></div><div class="fe-panel fe-sending"><div><p class="fe-eyebrow fe-label"><i class="fa fa-paper-plane" aria-hidden="true"></i>Unlimited sending</p><h3 class="fe-display-sm fe-sending__title">Unlimited outbound email, earned by reputation.</h3><p class="fe-text fe-sending__body">Send with SMTP or the API on any paid plan from $3/mo. New senders start at 100 messages per day (300 on the Team plan), and the daily threshold grows as you keep paying and send clean mail. According to <a href="https://support.google.com/a/answer/81126" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Gmail's sender guidelines</a>, senders should keep their spam rate under 0.1%. We use a similar limit: a day when large mailbox providers reject mail to 0.1% or more of your recipients as spam or viruses can lower your threshold one step.</p><a class="fe-feature__link" href="/en/faq#what-are-your-outbound-smtp-limits">How sending thresholds work</a></div><div><ol class="fe-sending__tiers" aria-label="Daily sending thresholds"><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">100</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">500</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">1,000</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">2,000</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">5,000</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral fe-sending__tier--top">10,000+</li></ol><p class="fe-sending__unit">messages per day, unlocked as your reputation grows</p></div></div></div></section><section class="fe-section fe-surface-light fe-section--pad fe-section--tight fe-pricing" aria-labelledby="fe-pricing-title"><div class="fe-container"><p class="fe-eyebrow fe-eyebrow--mint fe-label"><i class="fa fa-tag" aria-hidden="true"></i>Simple pricing</p><div class="fe-pricing__head"><h2 class="fe-display-lg" id="fe-pricing-title">How many domains can I use?</h2><a class="fe-pricing__aside" href="/en/private-business-email">Recommended for most professionals</a></div><p class="fe-lede">Every plan includes unlimited domains, the free plan too. Foundation</a> across 250+ domains, and we built the @ubuntu.com member addresses for <a href="/en/blog/docs/canonical-ubuntu-email-enterprise-case-study">Canonical</a>.</p></div></li></ul></div></section><section class="fe-section fe-surface-light fe-section--pad fe-proof" id="testimonials" aria-labelledby="fe-proof-title"><div class="fe-container"><p class="fe-eyebrow fe-label">Testimonials</p><h2 class="fe-display-lg" id="fe-proof-title">Why do people switch to Forward Email?</h2><p class="fe-lede">Customers give three reasons: privacy built into the architecture, a migration that takes a few DNS changes, and one inbox for every address. Each quote links to proof you can check. <a href="https://cure53.de/" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Cure53</a> spent 25 days auditing our code, our infrastructure and Nodemailer, with four senior testers and full access to the source, according to its report.</p><div class="fe-proof__grid"><figure class="fe-panel fe-panel--quiet fe-proof__card fe-proof__card--featured"><p class="fe-proof__pillar fe-label"><span class="fe-proof__dot fe-proof__dot--primary" aria-hidden="true"></span>Private by architecture</p><blockquote class="fe-proof__quote">Congrats for fully launching Forward Email – a forwarding service␊
    for email that doesn't keep logs or store emails, and which works with ARC to ensure␊
    signed forwards don't trip email filters 👏. I'm a happy user! ❤️</blockquote><figcaption class="fe-proof__who"><img class="fe-proof__avatar" loading="eager" fetchpriority="low" decoding="async" width="44" height="44" src="http://example.com:3000/img/testimonials/dhh-44-HASH.webp" srcset="http://example.com:3000/img/testimonials/dhh-44-HASH.webp 1x, http://example.com:3000/img/testimonials/dhh-HASH.webp 2x" alt="DHH"/><div class="fe-proof__id"><span class="fe-proof__name">DHH</span><span class="fe-proof__meta">@dhh · Creator of Ruby on Rails, Founder &amp; CTO at Basecamp &amp; HEY</span></div></figcaption><a class="fe-proof__receipt" href="/pentest-report_forward-email.pdf">Read the security audit <i class="fa fa-angle-right" aria-hidden="true"></i></a></figure><figure class="fe-panel fe-panel--quiet fe-proof__card"><p class="fe-proof__pillar fe-label"><span class="fe-proof__dot fe-proof__dot--mint" aria-hidden="true"></span>Migration is a DNS change</p><blockquote class="fe-proof__quote">Have now switched email forwarding from MailGun to ForwardEmail.net␊
    . Simple and painless (and free!). Just some DNS changes, and it just works. Thanks</blockquote><figcaption class="fe-proof__who"><img class="fe-proof__avatar" loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="44" height="44" src="http://example.com:3000/img/testimonials/abhinemani-44-HASH.webp" srcset="http://example.com:3000/img/testimonials/abhinemani-44-HASH.webp 1x, http://example.com:3000/img/testimonials/abhinemani-HASH.webp 2x" alt="abhi nemani"/><div class="fe-proof__id"><span class="fe-proof__name">abhi nemani</span><span class="fe-proof__meta">@abhinemani · Government Technology Advisor, Sacramento and Los Angeles</span></div></figcaption><a class="fe-proof__receipt" href="/en/guides">Browse the setup guides <i class="fa fa-angle-right" aria-hidden="true"></i></a></figure><figure class="fe-panel fe-panel--quiet fe-proof__card"><p class="fe-proof__pillar fe-label"><span class="fe-proof__dot fe-proof__dot--signal" aria-hidden="true"></span>One inbox for every address</p><blockquote class="fe-proof__quote">So they made this cool app that forwards email from your own domain to your Gmail inbox.         {␊
          "@type": "Question",␊
          "name": "Is Forward Email better than Proton Mail or Tuta Mail?",␊
          "acceptedAnswer": {␊
            "@type": "Answer",␊
            "text": "Forward Email is the only email service that is 100% open-source (both front-end and back-end). Unlike Proton Mail and Tuta Mail, Forward Email supports standard IMAP/POP3/SMTP protocols, works with any email client, offers unlimited custom domains on all plans, and provides a free tier with unlimited email forwarding."␊
          }␊
        },␊
        {␊
          "@type": "Question",␊
          "name": "What is the most secure email provider?",␊
          "acceptedAnswer": {␊
            "@type": "Answer",␊
            "text": "Forward Email uses quantum-resistant ChaCha20-Poly1305 encryption, zero-knowledge architecture, and supports all modern email security standards including SPF, DKIM, DMARC, BIMI, MTA-STS, DANE, and TLS 1.3. The entire codebase is open-source and auditable on GitHub."␊
          }␊
        }␊
      ]␊
    }␊

Nada del correo es legible para nosotros.</p><ul class="fe-grid fe-grid--2 fe-console__cards"><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">IMAP · POP3</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Apple Mail, Thunderbird, mobile</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">10 GB pooled</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Shared across every domain</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">Zero-knowledge</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Cifrado resistente a la cuántica</span></li><li class="fe-console__card"><span class="fe-console__card-label">Search &amp; filters</span><span class="fe-console__card-sub">Búsqueda completa, del lado del servidor, sin descifrar nunca tu correo</span></li></ul><p class="fe-console__evidence fe-code"><span class="fe-console__evidence-glyph" aria-hidden="true">✓</span><span>imap.forwardemail.net · TLS 1.3 ·</span><span>mailbox sealed</span></p></section><section class="fe-console__view fe-console__view--signal" id="send" aria-labelledby="view-send-title" data-fe-console-view="send"><p class="fe-console__kicker"><span class="fe-console__dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>Enviar</p><h2 class="fe-console__heading" id="view-send-title">Outbound SMTP &amp; newsletters</h2><p class="fe-text fe-console__promise">Envía desde tu dominio desde una aplicación, un script o una campaña. Cure53 ha auditado nuestro código dos veces.</p><ul class="fe-chips"><li class="fe-chip"><i class="fab fa-github" aria-hidden="true"></i>Código abierto</li><li class="fe-chip"><i class="fa fa-lock fe-chip__icon--success" aria-hidden="true"></i>Reenvío PGP</li><li class="fe-chip"><i class="fa fa-shield-alt fe-chip__icon--primary" aria-hidden="true"></i>Protección contra spam y phishing</li></ul></div><div class="fe-feature fe-feature--inverse fe-surface-dark"><h3 class="fe-display-sm fe-feature__title">Diseñado para desarrolladores</h3><p class="fe-text fe-feature__body">Todo lo que hace el panel es una llamada a la API. Envia con una sola solicitud, recibe como webhooks JSON y automatiza el resto. Nuestro webmail y nuestras aplicaciones usan la misma API. <a class="fe-feature__link" href="/es/email-api">Lee la referencia de la API</a></p><div class="fe-codeblock"><pre><code><span class="fe-tok-method">POST /v1/emails</span>␊
    <span class="fe-tok-key">{ "from"</span><span class="fe-tok-value">: "you@domain.com",</span> <span class="fe-tok-key">"to"</span><span class="fe-tok-value">: "customer@co.com" }</span>␊
    <span class="fe-tok-result">→ 202 Accepted · signed · tracked</span></code></pre></div><ul class="fe-chips"><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">REST API</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">SMTP</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">IMAP</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">POP3</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">CalDAV</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">CardDAV</li><li class="fe-chip fe-chip--code">Webhooks</li></ul></div></div><div class="fe-panel fe-sending"><div><p class="fe-eyebrow fe-label"><i class="fa fa-paper-plane" aria-hidden="true"></i>Envíos ilimitados</p><h3 class="fe-display-sm fe-sending__title">Correo saliente ilimitado, ganado con reputación.</h3><p class="fe-text fe-sending__body">Envíe con SMTP o la API en cualquier plan de pago desde $3/mes. Los nuevos remitentes comienzan con 100 mensajes por día (300 en el plan Team), y el umbral diario aumenta mientras siga pagando y envíe correo limpio. Según <a href="https://support.google.com/a/answer/81126" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">las directrices para remitentes de Gmail</a>, los remitentes deben mantener su tasa de spam por debajo del 0,1 %. Usamos un límite similar: un día en que los grandes proveedores de buzones rechazan como spam o virus el correo dirigido al 0,1 % o más de sus destinatarios puede bajar su umbral un nivel.</p><a class="fe-feature__link" href="/es/faq#what-are-your-outbound-smtp-limits">Cómo funcionan los umbrales de envío</a></div><div><ol class="fe-sending__tiers" aria-label="Umbrales de envío diarios"><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">100</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">500</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">1,000</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">2,000</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral">5,000</li><li class="fe-chip fe-numeral fe-sending__tier--top">10,000+</li></ol><p class="fe-sending__unit">mensajes por día, que se desbloquean a medida que crece su reputación</p></div></div></div></section><section class="fe-section fe-surface-light fe-section--pad fe-section--tight fe-pricing" aria-labelledby="fe-pricing-title"><div class="fe-container"><p class="fe-eyebrow fe-eyebrow--mint fe-label"><i class="fa fa-tag" aria-hidden="true"></i>Simple pricing</p><div class="fe-pricing__head"><h2 class="fe-display-lg" id="fe-pricing-title">¿Cuántos dominios puedo usar?</h2><a class="fe-pricing__aside" href="/es/private-business-email">Recomendado para la mayoría de profesionales</a></div><p class="fe-lede">Todos los planes incluyen dominios ilimitados, también el plan gratuito.     <h2 id="disclaimer" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#disclaimer" role="button" aria-label=" Disclaimer" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-disclaimer" data-target="#collapse-disclaimer"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Disclaimer</h2>␊
    <p>By accessing this web site, you are agreeing to be bound by these Terms and Conditions of Use, all applicable laws and regulations, and agree that you are responsible for compliance with any applicable local laws.</p>␊
    <p>You must be at least eighteen (18) years of age, or between thirteen (13) and seventeen (17) years of age and using the Service with parental or legal guardian consent and supervision.</p>␊

    <p>The materials on Forward Email's web site and related services (&quot;Service&quot;) are provided &quot;as is&quot;.</p>␊
    <p>Forward Email makes no warranties, expressed or implied, and hereby disclaims and negates all other warranties, including without limitation, implied warranties or conditions of merchantability, fitness for a particular purpose, or non-infringement of intellectual property or other violation of rights.</p>␊
    <p>Further, Forward Email does not warrant or make any representations concerning the accuracy, likely results, or reliability of the use of the materials on its Service or otherwise relating to such materials or on any sites linked to this site.</p>␊

    <p><u><strong>You specifically agree that by using the Service that Forward Email shall not be responsible for, shall not be liable for, and you will not create, read, modify, or transmit any of the following with the Service:</strong></u></p>␊
    <ul>␊
    <li><strong>You will not use our service to operate a service which allow third parties (other than your own employees and contractors) to access, use, or benefit from our service.  If you need us to make an exception for you regarding this, then please contact us to discuss an enterprise agreement.  See our <a href="https://github.com/forwardemail/forwardemail.net/blob/master/LICENSE.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">LICENSE</a> for more information.</strong></li>␊
    <li>If you are using our <a href="/faq#do-you-support-sending-email-with-smtp">Outbound SMTP</a> service, then you agree to the following:␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>You are permitted to use it for <a href="https://wikipedia.org/wiki/Email_marketing#Transactional_emails" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transactional email</a> and <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Email_marketing" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">email marketing</a>.</li>␊
    <li>If you are sending email marketing material, then you must include a <code>List-Unsubscribe</code> header and your subscribers must be opt-in.␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>We recommend that you use an open-source newsletter manager such as <a href="https://github.com/knadh/listmonk" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ListMonk</a> at <a href="https://github.com/knadh/listmonk" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/knadh/listmonk</a>.␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>Note that we plan to release our own newsletter manager in the future.</li>␊
    </ul>␊
    </li>␊
    <li>Email marketing material includes promotional, bulk, or commercial email to a list of contacts with similar content, subject line, and body message (e.g. newsletters and announcements).</li>␊
    <li>You must comply with the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CAN-SPAM_Act_of_2003" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">CAN-SPAM Act of 2003</a>.</li>␊
    <li>You must remove users that have opted-out, unsubscribed, or marked your email as spam either instantly or within 24 hours.</li>␊
    <li>The first-time your domain name attempts to send a newsletter, an admin will review and approve your domain for sending newsletters (usually within 2-4 hours, but sometimes it may take longer).</li>␊
    </ul>␊
    </li>␊
    </ul>␊
    </li>␊

    <h2 id="unlimited-usage" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#unlimited-usage" role="button" aria-label=" Unlimited Usage" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-unlimited-usage" data-target="#collapse-unlimited-usage"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Unlimited Usage</h2>␊
    <p>Regarding &quot;unlimited&quot; usage in our service, we automatically rate limit and may temporarily restrict or slow usage when necessary to maintain quality of service for all customers.</p>␊
    <p>If we determine your usage of services differs from normal user behavior, is deemed to be spam or malicious activity by our team, or for any other reason under our sole discretion – then we may restrict or slow usage.</p>␊
    <p>We may or may not notify you in advance of restrictions already imposed or that will be imposed on your account and the service in general.</p>␊
    <h2 id="limitations" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#limitations" role="button" aria-label=" Limitations" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-limitations" data-target="#collapse-limitations"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Limitations</h2>␊
    <p>In no event shall Forward Email or its suppliers be liable for any damages (including, without limitation, damages for loss of data or profit, or due to business interruption,) arising out of the use or inability to use the materials on Forward Email's Internet site, even if Forward Email or a Forward Email authorized representative has been notified orally or in writing of the possibility of such damage. Because some jurisdictions do not allow limitations on implied warranties, or limitations of liability for consequential or incidental damages, these limitations may not apply to you.</p>␊
    <h2 id="refunds" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#refunds" role="button" aria-label=" Refunds" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-refunds" data-target="#collapse-refunds"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Refunds</h2>␊
    <p>Automatic refunds occur when you upgrade, downgrade, or cancel your account within 30-days from when your plan first started.  This only applies for first-time customers.</p>␊
    <p>We do not pro-rate nor refund the difference when you switch plans. Instead we convert the remaining duration from your existing plan's expiration date into the closest relative duration for your new plan (rounded down by month).</p>␊
    <p>Note that if you upgrade or downgrade between paid plans within a 30-day window since first starting a paid plan, then we will automatically refund the full amount from your existing plan.</p>␊
    <h2 id="disputes" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#disputes" role="button" aria-label=" Disputes" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-disputes" data-target="#collapse-disputes"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Disputes</h2>␊
    <p>Please contact us if you would like to dispute a transaction or charge from our service.  Do not contact your credit card provider, bank, or PayPal.</p>␊
    <p>Your account will be automatically and permanently banned if you open a dispute with your credit card provider, bank, or PayPal.</p>␊
    <h2 id="service-level-agreement-sla" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#service-level-agreement-sla" role="button" aria-label=" Service Level Agreement (&quot;SLA&quot;)" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-service-level-agreement-sla" data-target="#collapse-service-level-agreement-sla"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Service Level Agreement (&quot;SLA&quot;)</h2>␊
    <h3 id="terms-1" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#terms-1" role="button" aria-label="Go to top"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Terms</h3>␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>&quot;Downtime&quot; refers to a factor of more than a 5% user error rate, determined by a server-side error rate.</li>␊
    <li>&quot;Monthly Uptime Percentage&quot; refers to the total minutes in a calendar month minus the number of minutes of Downtime suffered in the calendar month, divided by the total minutes in the calendar month.</li>␊
    </ul>␊
    <h3 id="agreement" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#agreement" role="button" aria-label="Go to top"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Agreement</h3>␊
    <p>Forward Email shall use all reasonable commercial efforts (no less than acceptable and reasonable industry standards) to ensure that the Forward Email service is available to paying customers 99.99% of the time in any calendar month.  If it is not, then you may be eligible to receive the <a href="#credits">Credits</a> described below in accordance with <a href="#eligibility">Eligibility</a>.</p>␊

    <h3 id="eligibility" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#eligibility" role="button" aria-label="Go to top"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Eligibility</h3>␊
    <p>Minimum credit amount must be at least $1.00 to be eligible.  Maximum credit is capped at the 30 days or the total amount the customer is paying per the most recent calendar month with respect to Downtime (whichever is greater).  Paying customers affected for any Downtime must request Credits by filing a <a href="/help">Help request</a> within 30 days from the date of Downtime.  Credits only apply for Downtime factors that are within the primary control of Forward Email.</p>␊

            <div class="markdown-body"><h1 id="terms" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#top" id="top" role="button" aria-label="Ir arriba"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Términos</h1>␊
    ␊
    ␊
    ␊
    <h2 id="disclaimer" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#disclaimer" role="button" aria-label=" Descargo de responsabilidad" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-disclaimer" data-target="#collapse-disclaimer"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Descargo de responsabilidad</h2>␊
    <p>Al acceder a este sitio web, usted acepta estar sujeto a estos Términos y Condiciones de Uso, todas las leyes y regulaciones aplicables, y acepta que es responsable del cumplimiento de cualquier ley local aplicable.</p>␊
    <p>Debe tener al menos dieciocho (18) años de edad, o estar entre trece (13) y diecisiete (17) años de edad y usar el Servicio con el consentimiento y supervisión de un padre o tutor legal.</p>␊
    <p><u><strong>Si no está de acuerdo con alguno de estos términos o con cualquiera de los términos a continuación, tiene prohibido usar o acceder a este sitio.</strong></u></p>␊
    <p>Los materiales en el sitio web de Forward Email y servicios relacionados (&quot;Servicio&quot;) se proporcionan &quot;tal cual&quot;.</p>␊
    <p>Forward Email no ofrece garantías, expresas o implícitas, y por la presente renuncia y niega todas las demás garantías, incluyendo sin limitación, garantías implícitas o condiciones de comerciabilidad, idoneidad para un propósito particular, o no infracción de propiedad intelectual u otra violación de derechos.</p>␊
    <p>Además, Forward Email no garantiza ni hace representaciones sobre la exactitud, resultados probables o confiabilidad del uso de los materiales en su Servicio o de otro modo relacionados con dichos materiales o en cualquier sitio vinculado a este sitio.</p>␊
    <p><u><strong>Usted acepta específicamente que no usará el Servicio para ningún negocio o materiales relacionados con ambos <a href="https://stripe.com/legal/restricted-businesses" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Negocios Prohibidos de Stripe</a> y <a href="https://www.paypal.com/us/legalhub/acceptableuse-full" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Actividades Prohibidas de PayPal</a>.</strong></u></p>␊
    <p><u><strong>Usted acepta específicamente que al usar el Servicio, Forward Email no será responsable, no tendrá ninguna responsabilidad, y usted no creará, leerá, modificará o transmitirá ninguno de los siguientes con el Servicio:</strong></u></p>␊
    <ul>␊
    <li><strong>No usará nuestro servicio para operar un servicio que permita a terceros (que no sean sus propios empleados y contratistas) acceder, usar o beneficiarse de nuestro servicio. Si necesita que hagamos una excepción para usted respecto a esto, por favor contáctenos para discutir un acuerdo empresarial. Vea nuestra <a href="https://github.com/forwardemail/forwardemail.net/blob/master/LICENSE.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">LICENSE</a> para más información.</strong></li>␊
    <li>Si está usando nuestro servicio <a href="/faq#do-you-support-sending-email-with-smtp">SMTP Saliente</a>, entonces acepta lo siguiente:␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>Se le permite usarlo para <a href="https://wikipedia.org/wiki/Email_marketing#Transactional_emails" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">correo electrónico transaccional</a> y <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Email_marketing" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marketing por correo electrónico</a>.</li>␊
    <li>Si está enviando material de marketing por correo electrónico, debe incluir un encabezado <code>List-Unsubscribe</code> y sus suscriptores deben haber optado por recibirlo.␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>Recomendamos que use un gestor de boletines de código abierto como <a href="https://github.com/knadh/listmonk" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ListMonk</a> en <a href="https://github.com/knadh/listmonk" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">https://github.com/knadh/listmonk</a>.␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>Tenga en cuenta que planeamos lanzar nuestro propio gestor de boletines en el futuro.</li>␊
    </ul>␊
    </li>␊
    <li>El material de marketing por correo electrónico incluye correo promocional, masivo o comercial a una lista de contactos con contenido, asunto y mensaje corporal similares (por ejemplo, boletines y anuncios).</li>␊
    <li>Debe cumplir con la <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CAN-SPAM_Act_of_2003" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Ley CAN-SPAM de 2003</a>.</li>␊
    <li>Debe eliminar a los usuarios que se hayan dado de baja, hayan cancelado la suscripción o hayan marcado su correo como spam, ya sea instantáneamente o dentro de 24 horas.</li>␊
    <li>La primera vez que su nombre de dominio intente enviar un boletín, un administrador revisará y aprobará su dominio para enviar boletines (usualmente dentro de 2-4 horas, pero a veces puede tardar más).</li>␊
    </ul>␊
    </li>␊
    </ul>␊
    </li>␊
    <li>Cualquier cosa que cause interferencia o interrupción del Servicio, sus operaciones y/o sus clientes.</li>␊
    <li>Materiales que sean abusivos, difamatorios, falsos, acosadores, indecentes, calumniosos, engañosos, objetables, obscenos, ofensivos, profanos, amenazantes, ilegales y/o vulgares.</li>␊
    <li>Materiales con derechos de autor no autorizados o confidenciales y/o materiales que infrinjan derechos de propiedad intelectual, secretos comerciales y/o privacidad de otros.</li>␊
    <li>Cualquier cosa que fomente conductas criminales, tenga el potencial de causar responsabilidad civil y/o viole leyes o regulaciones de cualquier jurisdicción.</li>␊
    <li>Materiales que causen daño o tengan el potencial de causar daño a menores, que se hagan pasar por otra persona o entidad, y/o que tergiversen una afiliación con una persona o entidad.</li>␊
    <li>Materiales que contengan spam, virus, malware, materiales NSFW (contenido indecente, provocativo o profano), archivos corruptos y/o cualquier otro software o programas que puedan dañar o tengan el potencial de causar daño a una persona, entidad, computadora, red, gobierno, servicio y/o cualquier otro asunto.␊
    Los materiales contenidos en este sitio web están protegidos por las leyes aplicables de derechos de autor y marcas registradas.</li>␊
    </ul>␊
    <p>Su acceso a nuestro sitio web y el uso de nuestro servicio indican que ha aceptado nuestra <a href="/privacy">Política de Privacidad</a> y el <a href="/dpa">Acuerdo de Procesamiento de Datos</a> (por ejemplo, para el cumplimiento del GDPR).</p>␊
    <h2 id="unlimited-usage" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#unlimited-usage" role="button" aria-label=" Uso Ilimitado" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-unlimited-usage" data-target="#collapse-unlimited-usage"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Uso Ilimitado</h2>␊
    <p>Con respecto al uso &quot;ilimitado&quot; en nuestro servicio, aplicamos límites automáticos de tasa y podemos restringir o ralentizar temporalmente el uso cuando sea necesario para mantener la calidad del servicio para todos los clientes.</p>␊
    <p>Si determinamos que su uso de los servicios difiere del comportamiento normal del usuario, se considera spam o actividad maliciosa por nuestro equipo, o por cualquier otra razón a nuestra sola discreción, entonces podemos restringir o ralentizar el uso.</p>␊
    <p>Podemos o no notificarle con anticipación sobre las restricciones ya impuestas o que se impondrán en su cuenta y en el servicio en general.</p>␊
    <h2 id="limitations" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#limitations" role="button" aria-label=" Limitaciones" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-limitations" data-target="#collapse-limitations"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Limitaciones</h2>␊
    <p>En ningún caso Forward Email ni sus proveedores serán responsables por daños (incluyendo, sin limitación, daños por pérdida de datos o ganancias, o debido a interrupción del negocio,) que surjan del uso o la imposibilidad de usar los materiales en el sitio de Internet de Forward Email, incluso si Forward Email o un representante autorizado de Forward Email ha sido notificado oralmente o por escrito de la posibilidad de tales daños. Debido a que algunas jurisdicciones no permiten limitaciones en garantías implícitas, o limitaciones de responsabilidad por daños consecuentes o incidentales, estas limitaciones pueden no aplicarse a usted.</p>␊
    <h2 id="refunds" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#refunds" role="button" aria-label=" Reembolsos" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-refunds" data-target="#collapse-refunds"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Reembolsos</h2>␊
    <p>Los reembolsos automáticos ocurren cuando actualiza, degrada o cancela su cuenta dentro de los 30 días desde que su plan comenzó por primera vez. Esto solo aplica para clientes primerizos.</p>␊
    <p>No prorrateamos ni reembolsamos la diferencia cuando cambia de plan. En su lugar, convertimos la duración restante desde la fecha de expiración de su plan actual a la duración relativa más cercana para su nuevo plan (redondeado hacia abajo por mes).</p>␊
    <p>Tenga en cuenta que si actualiza o degrada entre planes pagos dentro de una ventana de 30 días desde que comenzó un plan pago, entonces reembolsaremos automáticamente el monto total de su plan actual.</p>␊
    <h2 id="disputes" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#disputes" role="button" aria-label=" Disputas" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-disputes" data-target="#collapse-disputes"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Disputas</h2>␊
    <p>Por favor contáctenos si desea disputar una transacción o cargo de nuestro servicio. No contacte a su proveedor de tarjeta de crédito, banco o PayPal.</p>␊
    <p>Su cuenta será automáticamente y permanentemente bloqueada si abre una disputa con su proveedor de tarjeta de crédito, banco o PayPal.</p>␊
    <h2 id="service-level-agreement-sla" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#service-level-agreement-sla" role="button" aria-label=" Acuerdo de Nivel de Servicio (&quot;SLA&quot;)" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-service-level-agreement-sla" data-target="#collapse-service-level-agreement-sla"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Acuerdo de Nivel de Servicio (&quot;SLA&quot;)</h2>␊
    <h3 id="terms-1" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#terms-1" role="button" aria-label="Ir arriba"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Términos</h3>␊
    <ul>␊
    <li>&quot;Tiempo de inactividad&quot; se refiere a un factor de más del 5% de tasa de error de usuario, determinado por una tasa de error del lado del servidor.</li>␊
    <li>&quot;Porcentaje de tiempo de actividad mensual&quot; se refiere al total de minutos en un mes calendario menos el número de minutos de Tiempo de inactividad sufrido en el mes calendario, dividido por el total de minutos en el mes calendario.</li>␊
    </ul>␊
    <h3 id="agreement" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#agreement" role="button" aria-label="Ir arriba"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Acuerdo</h3>␊
    <p>Forward Email hará todos los esfuerzos comerciales razonables (no menos que los estándares aceptables y razonables de la industria) para asegurar que el servicio de Forward Email esté disponible para clientes que pagan el 99.99% del tiempo en cualquier mes calendario. Si no es así, usted puede ser elegible para recibir los <a href="#credits">Créditos</a> descritos a continuación de acuerdo con la <a href="#eligibility">Elegibilidad</a>.</p>␊
    <h3 id="credits" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#credits" role="button" aria-label="Ir arriba"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Créditos</h3>␊
    <table>␊
    <thead>␊
    <tr>␊
    <th>Porcentaje de tiempo de actividad mensual</th>␊
    <th>Días de servicio*</th>␊
    </tr>␊
    </thead>␊
    <tbody>␊
    <tr>␊
    <td>&lt; 99.99% – &gt;= 99.90%</td>␊
    <td>3</td>␊
    </tr>␊
    <tr>␊
    <td>&lt; 99.90% – &gt;= 95.00%</td>␊
    <td>7</td>␊
    </tr>␊
    <tr>␊
    <td>&lt; 95.50%</td>␊
    <td>15</td>␊
    </tr>␊
    </tbody>␊
    </table>␊
    <p>* En lugar de recibir Días de Servicio añadidos a su cuenta, también puede elegir que le emitamos un reembolso prorrateado.</p>␊
    <h3 id="eligibility" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#eligibility" role="button" aria-label="Ir arriba"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Elegibilidad</h3>␊
    <p>El monto mínimo de crédito debe ser al menos $1.00 para ser elegible. El crédito máximo está limitado a 30 días o al monto total que el cliente paga según el mes calendario más reciente con respecto al Tiempo de inactividad (lo que sea mayor). Los clientes que pagan afectados por cualquier Tiempo de inactividad deben solicitar Créditos presentando una <a href="/help">solicitud de ayuda</a> dentro de los 30 días desde la fecha del Tiempo de inactividad. Los créditos solo aplican para factores de Tiempo de inactividad que estén bajo el control principal de Forward Email.</p>␊
    <h2 id="revisions-and-errata" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#revisions-and-errata" role="button" aria-label=" Revisiones y Erratas" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-revisions-and-errata" data-target="#collapse-revisions-and-errata"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Revisiones y Erratas</h2>␊
    <p>Los materiales que aparecen en el sitio web de Forward Email podrían incluir errores técnicos, tipográficos o fotográficos. Forward Email no garantiza que ninguno de los materiales en su sitio web sea exacto, completo o actual. Forward Email puede realizar cambios en los materiales contenidos en su sitio web en cualquier momento sin previo aviso. Sin embargo, Forward Email no se compromete a actualizar los materiales.</p>␊
    <h2 id="links" tabindex="-1"><a class="anchor" href="#links" role="button" aria-label=" Enlaces" data-toggle="collapse" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="collapse-links" data-target="#collapse-links"><svg aria-hidden="true" class="octicon octicon-link" height="16" version="1.1" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16"><path d="M4 9h1v1H4c-1.5 0-3-1.69-3-3.5S2.55 3 4 3h4c1.45 0 3 1.69 3 3.5 0 1.41-.91 2.72-2 3.25V8.59c.58-.45 1-1.27 1-2.09C10 5.22 8.98 4 8 4H4c-.98 0-2 1.22-2 2.5S3 9 4 9zm9-3h-1v1h1c1 0 2 1.22 2 2.5S13.98 12 13 12H9c-.98 0-2-1.22-2-2.5 0-.83.42-1.64 1-2.09V6.25c-1.09.53-2 1.84-2 3.25C6 11.31 7.55 13 9 13h4c1.45 0 3-1.69 3-3.5S14.5 6 13 6z"></path></svg></a> Enlaces</h2>␊
    <p>Forward Email no ha revisado todos los sitios vinculados a su Servicio y no es responsable del contenido de dichos sitios vinculados. La inclusión de cualquier enlace no implica el respaldo por parte de Forward Email del sitio. El uso de cualquier sitio web vinculado es bajo el propio riesgo del usuario.</p>␊
